# P16a — old QMA deletion plan

Inspected `rebuild/base` commit `74f550bb16baadd87ef528786284daa29a31b17d` on 2026-10-09. Report only: **zero source modules deleted and zero source LOC removed**. This report is the explicit P16a exception to README’s general no-report rule. Merging it does not approve P16b; stop for Claude’s review.

## Scope and counting

Only Python runtime modules under the three source roots below are deletion candidates. A module is one tracked `.py` file, including initializers and bundled pack entrypoints. LOC is physical lines including comments and blanks (`len(path.read_bytes().splitlines())`), not executable statements. Tests, examples, metadata, schemas and other resources are consumers or retained assets, excluded from the module/LOC totals. No whole-directory deletion is authorized.

| Package | Delete candidates | Candidate LOC | Keep modules | Keep LOC | Total modules | Total LOC |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| qma-daemon | 107 | 57,595 | 79 | 31,401 | 186 | 88,996 |
| qma-core | 39 | 12,737 | 35 | 12,249 | 74 | 24,986 |
| qma-wire | 20 | 7,022 | 24 | 10,663 | 44 | 17,685 |
| **Total** | 166 | 77,354 | 138 | 54,313 | 304 | 131,667 |

**166 modules / 77,354 LOC are conditional deletion candidates; 138 modules / 54,313 LOC stay.** These are eventual candidate totals, not a claim that those files are safe to remove today. Any listed retained caller, out-of-scope caller, unported rule or unmigrated state blocks deletion. No delete-now batch is certified. Keep is conservative retention for this plan, not a recommendation to preserve a second QMA runtime.

`packages/qmf-*`, `qml/`, `qmb/`, `qmn/` (including MIS), `extensions/`, `apps/` and `docs/` are outside the deletion boundary. Read-only caller searches include them. Package moves, imports, manifests, tests, lockfiles and source changes are not performed in P16a. `qmx-agents/plugins/`, `qa/`, scripts and tooling are also outside the three candidate roots: list their references, do not silently add them to the deletion batch.

## Evidence and limitations

- Enumerated tracked files with `git ls-files`; parsed all **2,187 Python files with Python 3.14: zero syntax errors**. Resolved absolute/relative imports, conditional/local imports, module loads and named re-exports. Recorded import sites rather than claiming that each runs in production.
- Searched every readable tracked UTF-8 text file for literal `qma.daemon`, `qma.core`, `qma.wire` identifiers and candidate source paths, including tests, examples, scripts, docs, manifests and configuration. Binary files are not code-reference evidence. P05 documentation references are marked as references, not executable callers.
- Inspected `python -m qma.daemon`, request-runtime entrypoints, build `module-name` declarations, plugin `daemon.plugin:activate` loading and the QMB schema name. The caller index preserves exact file/line sites; package re-exports and package initialization are included separately.
- Static analysis cannot enumerate arbitrary computed strings, environment-configured factories, installed plugins or callers outside Git. `QMX_TOOLS_FACTORY` in `qmx_tools.service.DomainContext.from_environment` is an example of an externally supplied module name. Unknown deployment use is not proof of dead code; P16b must inspect active configuration and rerun the inventory at its actual base.
- P05 described 306 modules / 131,901 LOC. This base has 304 / 131,667: `D:request_runtime/authored_graphs.py` and `D:request_runtime/memory_extraction.py` are already absent. They are not deletion candidates here. No strict `pytest.mark.xfail` or `pytest.xfail` declarations were found in the current Python tree; the only `xfail` text from the repo-wide search is test-result accounting in `tools/test_all.py`. Do not invent an xfail-removal batch.

## Replacements actually present

These are inspected implementation destinations, not certifications of one-for-one legacy behavior. P17–P22 additions already on the base are included where relevant. All old JSON-RPC bridge language and `AsyncSubAgent` recommendations in P05 are superseded by DESIGN §§12–12b.

| Area | Current package and entrypoints | Remaining deletion gate |
|---|---|---|
| Agents, tools, sessions | `qmx_agents.spec.build_agent`, `mcp_configuration`; `qmx_agents.copilot.build_copilot`, `stream_turn`; `qmx_tools.registry.default_tool_registry` | Re-home useful research/actor rules and retained scope checks; old callers have not automatically migrated. |
| Workflows and retries | `qmx_agents.workflows.compiler.compile`, `as_subagent`; `WorkflowRuns.start`, `resume`, `cancel` | Old graph/lease records are used by Experiment Ledger and QMB study tests; checkpoint replacement is not state conversion. |
| Models/native proxy | `qmx_agents.router.ModelRouter.resolve`, `resolve_model`, `router_middleware`; `credential` | Cut over native/proxy consumers and credentials; preserve in-flight state or explicitly drain it. |
| Memory/context | `StoreMemoryProvider.from_home`, `MemoryManager.middleware`, `summarization` | Preserve scoped/global approval behavior and move old memories; no LangMem and no alternate memory runtime. |
| Guards/hooks | `RunJournal.append`, `quarantine`; `MoneyPathMiddleware`; `AdmissionGate.stage`, `approve`, `check`; `AdmissionBackend`; `AppHooksMiddleware` | Preserve immutable evidence and rejected bytes; **no new ExperimentLedgerStore equivalent was found**. |
| Background bus/jobs | `BackgroundTasks.tools` creates `start_task`, `check_task`, `cancel_task`, `list_tasks`; `ControlService.record_work`, `runs_cancel`, `run_job` | No AsyncSubAgent/Agent Protocol server. Mailbox delivery and old cancellation/lease semantics require explicit caller cutover. |
| Apps/road/storage | `Supervisor.install`, `start`, `stop`, `call`; `Road.register`, `evidence`, `advance`, `publish`; `History.accept`, `append`; `Artifacts.save`, `accept`; `backup`, `restore` | Existing new code is not proof of every old MA02–MA04 replay/CAS/reference rule or old database migration. Keep useful rules until covered. |
| REST/MCP and host | `qmx_control.server.create_app`, `create_mcp`, `local_token`; `qmx_control.__main__.main` | New CLI has serve/start/status/stop/weekly-backup, not QMB’s full pair/configure/rotate/recover/placement protocol. |
| QMB/QMN domain doors | `DomainContext.run_backtest`; `qmx_tools.servers.backtest`, `node`; `ControlService.memoized_backtest` | P09’s in-process QMB run call is not a port of all old CLI result validation/cancellation/Experiment Ledger behavior. QMN remains a read/propose-only domain. |

## Highest-risk blockers and scope decisions

1. **QMB host retirement is blocked.** `qmb/src/qmb/doors/cli/host.py:110` sets `DAEMON_SPAWN_MODULE = "qma.daemon"`; lines 698–699 build the spawn command. It also owns `.daemon_writer`, host-control readiness, pair/configure/rotate/recover and placement behavior. `qmx_control.__main__.main` exists but does not implement that protocol. Changing a spawn constant is not enough. P16’s import-only allowance in QMB cannot authorize this behavioral rewrite: require a separately approved caller-migration packet before deleting `D:__main__.py`, `process.py` or host control. Do not build an old-wire compatibility bridge.
2. **Experiment Ledger versus discarded leases.** Keep `D:ledgers/experiment.py`, `experiments/service.py`, `experiments/sqlite.py`, `hooks/ledger_gate.py` and their durable evidence rules. `experiment.py` imports old `ActorId`, `HookResult`, `named_lease_kind`, `DispatchLease`, cancellation authority and journal machinery. `RunJournal.quarantine` preserves rejected bytes but does not replace the notebook. Task/Quant ledgers, fold registries, store-class ownership and governed-variable registries are intentional drops; extract experiment identity/authorization to run/app IDs before removing those dependencies. Mixed `journal/stores.py`, `ledgers/announcements.py` and `desk_views.py` stay until their retained experiment/evidence role is separated; do not port their Task/Quant tables.
3. **QMB’s money-path schema is a non-import contract.** `qmb/src/qmb/analysis/variants.py:66` names `qma.wire.money_path_field_diff.v1`; the wire validator and `schema_data/money_path_field_diff.v1.schema.json` still exist. Keep `W:money_path_diff.py`, `W:schemas.py` and the resource set. This identifier is not a Python import line and must not be casually renamed in an out-of-scope package. Preserve the field-level proof contract until an approved owner migration exists.
4. **Retained modules import deletion candidates.** Every candidate’s caller section includes a separate retained-caller list. Old package initializers still re-export obsolete machinery. Preserve initializers with kept children and trim exports only with their callers. An all-at-once removal of the candidate set would break the retained code; the table is a dependency-gated plan.
5. **Mini-app publication and historical storage.** New Road/History/Artifacts exist, but old publication validates immutable proof bytes, current owner/context, CAS, replay and committed-but-not-reconciled failure semantics. Keep old invariant-bearing source until new-owner tests demonstrate the needed rules and data is exportable. Never delete old journals, ledgers, SQLite files, artifact receipts, app volumes or credentials as part of source cleanup.
6. **Dynamic plugins and non-candidate consumers.** `D:plugins/packs.py:129` resolves `daemon.plugin:activate` from manifests using `spec_from_file_location` (line 152), and imports backtest service dynamically (line 243). Bundled packs stay. The separate `qmx-agents/plugins/*/daemon/plugin.py` copies also import core hooks/model/memory/enums; they are outside candidate roots and block wholesale core retirement. QA/QMB tests and the QMN example are real static consumers; only approved import retargeting may touch protected trees, with substantive test rewrites handled separately.
7. **Fixtures are not dead by label.** `plugins/lifecycle.py`, `sessions/chrome.py`, `staging/change_request.py`, kit journeys and `W:grant_mismatch_fixture.py` have production/re-export callers. Keep lifecycle rules; fixture candidates remain blocked until their callers move. Do not delete whole test directories or weaken coverage.

## Reading the module tables

| Prefix | Exact repository-relative root |
|---|---|
| `D:` | `qmx-agents/packages/qma-daemon/src/qma/daemon/` |
| `C:` | `qmx-agents/packages/qma-core/src/qma/core/` |
| `W:` | `qmx-agents/packages/qma-wire/src/qma/wire/` |
| `DT:` | `qmx-agents/packages/qma-daemon/tests/` |
| `CT:` | `qmx-agents/packages/qma-core/tests/` |
| `WT:` | `qmx-agents/packages/qma-wire/tests/` |

Each row has an exhaustive **observed** caller/reference section in the index below. `I` = direct import/from-submodule; `X` = named re-export resolved to this module; `L` = Python package initialization dependency; `E` = module/manifest entrypoint or inspected dynamic loader; `R` = literal identifier/path reference (includes strings, monkeypatch targets and documentation, not necessarily executable). Numbers are one-based lines at the inspected base. Following a direct/re-export edge shows callers through package facades; arbitrary runtime-generated names remain unknown. Prefixes abbreviate paths, not scopes for deletion.

Every module below is outside this plan’s deletion list. Porting retained rules later may allow a revised plan to retire their old files; it does not authorize blanket moves now. The keep list is intentionally broader than the final target runtime because behavior/data migration is not established merely by finding a new class with a similar name.

## Later deletion sequence and acceptance gates (not executed)

1. Resolve the QMB host behavioral migration and the out-of-root plugin/test consumers in their own authorized work. Port the Experiment Ledger rules and preserve historical evidence first. For any retained module, replace dependencies before deleting a candidate it imports.
2. After Claude approves the plan, take P16b slices in packet order: task graph/canonical join; hooks; proxy/native sessions; bus; memory; transport. Re-run callers for each slice; zero production callers outside that slice, including kept modules, is required. A planned replacement name does not satisfy this check.
3. Retarget tests that protect surviving behavior, then retire only tests uniquely tied to approved removed behavior. If strict-xfail tests reappear on the actual base, remove them only with their covered code. Do not remove or rewrite protected QMB/QMN suites beyond separately authorized import changes.
4. Handle package moves/metadata and regenerate locks after caller cutover. Preserve the package resources and kept source listed here. No old data files are deletion targets.
5. P16b must run `just test-all`, show actual removed LOC, and demonstrate QMB/QMN suites unaffected plus a gone or thin daemon. This report cannot satisfy those runtime acceptance criteria. Reticle is inapplicable to this report-only change; no app behavior changes.

## P16a validation

- Reconciled **304 unique module rows**, exactly once across delete and keep; **131,667 physical LOC**. All candidates are in the three permitted source roots; no tests/examples or protected packages enter the deletion set.
- All replacement symbols in the replacement summary were checked in current source. AST caller scan parsed 2,187 Python files with zero errors; this is source analysis, not runtime test execution.
- Only `rebuild/reports/p16-deletion-plan.md` is intended to change. `git diff --check` and report inventory/link/count checks are the local acceptance checks.
